### 2026-07-17 17:45:17  #公司

【天域生物：實際控制人羅衛國因違規減持擬被沒收違法所得1585萬元並罰款1975萬元】天域生物公告，公司於2026年1月1日披露了實際控制人羅衛國收到立案告知書事項。2026年7月17日，公司獲悉羅衛國收到重慶證監局下發的《行政處罰事先告知書》。經查，羅衛國為規避上市公司大股東減持比例限制，透過虛構基金份額轉讓交易方式隱匿一致行動關係，2021年11月15日至12月28日期間，其與一致行動人透過二級市場競價減持公司股票超總股本1%，違規減持1167.77萬股，違規減持金額約1.65億元，違法所得1585.33萬元。重慶證監局擬決定：對羅衛國給予警告，沒收違法所得1585.33萬元，並處以1975萬元罰款。該事項僅涉及實際控制人個人，與上市公司無關，不會對生產經營產生重大影響，亦不觸及重大違法強制退市及其他風險警示情形。

【聚變產業協會發布《2026全球聚變產業報告》指出，全球聚變產業邁入商業化落地關鍵期】聚變產業協會（Fusion Industry Association，FIA）釋出《2026全球聚變產業報告》。報告顯示，過去一年全球 56 傢俬營聚變企業新增融資 44.8 億美元，行業累計融資總額至 142.4 億美元。私人資本累計投入132.6 億美元，取代政府科研資金成為主導力量，行業競爭不再侷限於技術可行性驗證，重點轉向搭建完整的能源商業體系。報告還指出，技術層面依舊呈現多元競爭，磁約束、慣性約束、磁慣性約束三大路線並行發展，尚未出現佔據絕對優勢的商業化技術方案。

【美國陸軍部署AI招聘助手，利用智慧問答提升徵募效率】據美國國防部7月16日訊息，美國陸軍推出人工智慧聊天機器人“Staff Sgt. Star”，用於幫助徵募人員快速獲取政策、法規和業務指導資訊，提高招聘工作效率。該工具整合於陸軍徵募資訊科技系統（Accessions Information Environment），基於經過篩選的徵募法規、政策檔案和系統資料進行訓練，可在數秒內檢索相關資訊，為徵募人員提供任務辦理、豁免申請等具體指導。目前，“Staff Sgt. Star”已服務約1.2萬名授權使用者，預計最早於11月擴大至約2.8萬名使用者。未來美軍將進一步拓展該工具功能，使其從資訊查詢助手發展為能夠執行任務的智慧化工具。

### 2026-07-17 17:40:49  #其他

【小紅書：近期關閉假冒仿冒賬號356個】7月17日，小紅書發文《清朗·整治賬號名稱資訊亂象專項》：自“清朗·整治賬號名稱資訊亂象”專項開展以來，小紅書平臺持續強化賬號名稱資訊全流程管理，堅決整治名稱冒充、身份偽裝、夾帶違規資訊、名稱資訊黑灰產等突出問題。賬號名稱資訊是使用者在平臺上的“第一張名片”，也是資訊真實性與社群信任的重要基礎。針對當前賬號名稱亂象，平臺從技術攔截、人工稽核、應急處置、生態引導多個維度協同發力，持續淨化平臺賬號名稱資訊生態，切實維護廣大使用者的合法權益。近段時間，平臺累計處置違規個人資料資訊3.1萬個，關閉假冒仿冒賬號356個，針對9722餘個賬號傳送限期15日完成認證的整改通知。(紅星新聞）

【US-Iran attacks escalate; which side most urgently needs a ceasefire?】US-Iran tit‑for‑tat strikes have accelerated, keeping oil markets, regional risk and US munitions inventories in focus. Iran faces deep economic strain from decades of US sanctions and recent fighting: per‑capita GDP has fallen from about $8,000 to $5,000 and oil exports have dropped from roughly 2.2m bpd to 1.5m bpd. A June memorandum briefly eased restrictions — 60‑day waivers and asset unfreezing pushed the rial up ~15% — but the US has reimposed sanctions this week. CSIS analysis says Iran has taken heavy military attrition: as of April 1 missile stocks down ~30% and drone stocks down ~60%, with significant damage to naval infrastructure and weapons production sites; strikes have continued, including recent attacks on Greater Tunb island. Oil jumped about 12% after the latest strikes amid renewed fears for Strait of Hormuz transit; roughly one‑fifth of global oil flows use the strait. US domestic politics and costs matter: US average gasoline rose from $2.98/gal pre‑conflict to a May peak of $4.63/gal, and a YouGov poll shows 57% of Americans view participation as wrong ahead of November midterms. US munitions consumption is also a constraint — CSIS reports four of seven main combat munitions were more than half expended in the first phase; replenishment under industrial measures would take months to years — and analysts warn key interceptors and cruise missiles are being consumed rapidly. Iran appears resilient: analysts say leadership views the fight as existential, drone production reportedly resumed after April ceasefire and could replenish stocks within months. Regionally, Gulf states are strengthening data‑sharing and early‑warning coordination. Market takeaway: elevated oil risk premium, potential for protracted disruption to regional supply, and near‑term pressure on US military inventories and political capital.

【花旗：瀾起科技強勁業績及股份回購有助穩定股價波動，維持“買入”評級】花旗發表研報指，瀾起科技公佈強勁的2026年上半年初步業績，第二季淨利潤介乎10.53億至12.53億元，同比增長66%至98%，分別高於市場普遍預期及該行預期達37%和42%，主要受到更高收入規模、毛利率擴張及投資收益推動。期內收入同比增長33%至18.74億元，超市場及該行預期分別6%及11%。其中互連晶片收入同比增長28%至16.94億元。DDR5 RCD出貨量持續增長，且Gen 3及Gen 4佔比進一步擴大。該行提到，雖然韓國檢察官因懷疑價格壟斷串謀而突擊搜查相關辦公室的訊息導致股價下挫，且調查仍在進行並可能引致股價進一步波動，但該行認為強勁的2026年業績預告，加上管理層公佈的3億至6億元A股股份回購計劃，應有助於穩定股價。花旗表示，瀾起科技H股為國際投資者參與中國及全球AI資料中心擴充套件提供了罕有機會，其強烈的AI主題獲得國際投資者青睞，導致H股較A股出現溢價。該行對其港股目標價設於305港元，對應預測2027年市盈率66倍，維持“買入”評級。

【Euro-area June inflation eases, ECB keeps policy flexibility】Euro-area inflation cooled slightly in June, matching the flash estimate: headline MoM -0.1%, led by energy disinflation (-1.8%). Energy YoY inflation fell to 8.5% from 10.8% in May. Food inflation slowed to 1.5% YoY (from 1.9%) and services to 3.2% (from 3.5%), leaving core inflation on a downward path. The print reduces near-term upside inflation risk and gives the ECB greater flexibility to wait before adjusting policy over the summer. Key risk: renewed Middle East tensions could lift oil and gas prices, raising summer price pressure and increasing the chance of second-round effects.

### 2026-07-17 17:13:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malta #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Malta Annual Inflation Eases to 2025-Lows — Malta's annual inflation rate eased to 2.0% in June 2026, marking the lowest level since February 2025, down from 2.1% in the previous month. The slowdown was mainly supported by easing price growth in food (1.8% vs 2.0%) and transport (2.6% vs 2.9%), followed by recreation, sport and culture (3.4% vs 3.9%), health (3.0% vs 3.1%) and restaurants and accommodation services (3.0% vs 3.6%). Meanwhile, deflation eased for clothing and footwear (-3.9% vs -4.3%) and information and communication (-1.6% vs -2.5%). In contrast, price growth accelerated for housing and utilities (2.8% vs 2.5%), furnishings (2.6% vs 2.4%), alcoholic beverages, tobacco and narcotics (1.6% vs 1.5%) and education services (3.2% vs 3.1%). Costs in insurance and financial services rebounded, increased 0.3% following a 0.2% drop. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.1%, easing from 1.4% in the previous month.

### 2026-07-17 17:13:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Eurozone Inflation Confirmed at Four-Month Low in June — Eurozone annual inflation was confirmed at 2.8% in June 2026, down from 3.2% in May and marking its lowest level since February, before the Iran war disrupted energy supplies and drove oil prices higher. While inflation continued to ease, it remained above the European Central Bank's 2.0% target. Energy inflation slowed sharply to 8.5% from 10.8%, while price growth also moderated for services, non-energy industrial goods, and food, alcohol, and tobacco. Core inflation, which excludes energy and food, eased to 2.4% from 2.6%. Among the bloc's largest economies, inflation slowed in Germany, France, Italy, and the Netherlands, while remaining unchanged at 3.6% in Spain.

【中信建投黃文濤：矽基延續高景氣，內需待政策接力】中信建投黃文濤釋出研報稱，6月資料表明，增長斜率有所改善，結構分化尚未扭轉。K型分化加深體現為四個層面：生產強於需求，外需強於內需，高技術強於傳統部門，短期融資強於長期融資。近期政策思路與當前矛盾的匹配度提高。政策主線聚焦於提升消費能力，盤活城市存量，建設現代基礎設施。城市更新和“六張網”有望託底投資，消費與就業政策則決定內需修復的持續性。其中，高技術製造、現代服務和相關外需繼續走強。工業和服務業生產同步加快。6月製造業PMI升至50.3%。上半年裝備製造業和高技術製造業增加值分別增長9.3%和13.3%。工業機器人、鋰離子電池和3D列印裝置保持高增，資訊通訊行業增長10.7%。同時，出口結構也進一步向技術產品傾斜。機電產品出口增長20.1%，佔出口總額超過六成。矽基生產力仍是產出、利潤和外需的主要增量。

### 2026-07-17 17:08:50  #市場 #國際

【瑞銀資產管理高管看空美債 認為德債是極佳的防禦性配置選擇】瑞銀資產管理的Kevin Zhao計劃做空美國國債，押注美國經濟表現強勁將削弱政府債券作為避險資產的吸引力。“我們仍希望逢高賣出，”這位瑞銀資管的全球主權及貨幣基金主管在接受採訪時稱。“美國仍然處於最有利的位置。它沒有受到能源衝擊影響，並且仍是人工智慧(AI)投資熱潮的最大受益者。”另一方面，他表示德國國債是極佳的防禦性配置選擇。他認為能源衝擊將對歐洲經濟增長造成更大打擊，而該地區從AI投資中獲益的程度相對有限，這提升了德債的吸引力。債券經理們如今越來越擔心，美國通脹加速的局面正在固化，侵蝕投資回報並導致利率居高不下。Zhao表示，近期美伊衝突升級導致油價飆升，對歐洲增長構成的威脅大於對通脹的推升作用。“我們仍看好歐洲債券，因為歐洲經濟並未從AI投資熱潮中受益多少，而能源成本上升正嚴重影響其經濟增長，”他指出。與歐洲相仿，亞洲也依賴能源進口。但Zhao表示，這種拖累將被AI領域的投資所抵消。他表示，如果較長期德國國債收益率再漲10個基點，“我們就會買入”。10年期德債收益率目前在3.12%，距離5月觸及的15年高點3.20%已不遠。Zhao指出，明年的法國和義大利大選將進一步提升德債吸引力，因為選舉將推高政治風險。

### 2026-07-17 17:06:41  #國際

【美伊戰事影響進一步外溢 巴基斯坦劃出“紅線”】
自美伊戰事爆發以來，巴基斯坦發揮了重要斡旋作用。然而，訊息人士指出，沙烏地阿拉伯與葉門胡塞武裝日前的互襲事件，令巴基斯坦面臨捲入衝突的巨大風險，巴基斯坦方面已向伊朗方面鄭重表態，對沙特的襲擊就是對巴方的襲擊，這是巴方“紅線”。
胡塞武裝是伊朗的盟友。路透社16日援引巴方官員的話報道，巴政府和軍方領導人已把其“紅線”立場傳遞至伊朗最高層。
報道說，自美伊戰事爆發以來，巴基斯坦很少如此接近被迫“選邊站”。巴基斯坦安全分析師穆罕默德·阿米爾·拉納說，巴方沒想到緊張局勢會“驟然升級”。
依據沙特和巴基斯坦2025年9月簽署的共同戰略防禦協議，第三方針對兩國任何一國的攻擊將被視為“對兩國的侵略”。本月13日，胡塞武裝以沙特空襲其控制的葉門首都薩那國際機場為由，向沙特西南部的艾卜哈國際機場發射導彈和無人機。依照美國媒體說法，這是雙方2022年以來最為嚴重的衝突。
截至目前，上述衝突被視為“單一事件”。外界擔心，一旦沙特與胡塞武裝戰火重燃，地區緊張局勢將升級，美伊戰事影響將進一步外溢。
巴基斯坦外交部發言人塔希爾·安德拉比16日說，巴方呼籲各方保持最大剋制，透過對話和外交手段解決問題。
據路透社報道，沙特和巴基斯坦簽署共同戰略防禦協議，反映出海灣國家對美國提供所謂“安全保障”愈發心存疑慮，看好巴基斯坦等國為替代選項。
另據路透社16日援引訊息人士的話報道，伊朗告訴胡塞武裝，若美國襲擊伊朗電力網路，胡塞武裝就封鎖曼德海峽。伊朗官方未對此作出回應。 
報道說，胡塞武裝已完成相應準備，在曼德海峽附近部署導彈和無人機。有訊息人士指出，封鎖曼德海峽“不難”，要擾亂該水域的航運“甚至不需要複雜的導彈”。
曼德海峽連線紅海和亞丁灣，是溝通大西洋、地中海和印度洋的“咽喉”，被稱為連線歐亞非三大洲的“水上走廊”。在霍爾木茲海峽航運已因美伊戰事嚴重受阻的情況下，曼德海峽如果也被阻塞，將再度衝擊全球能源供應。
為繞開受阻的霍爾木茲海峽，沙烏地阿拉伯國家石油公司（沙特阿美）利用一條橫貫東西的輸油管道，把沙特東部產油區生產的原油輸送到紅海沿岸的延布港，再經曼德海峽出口。專業機構提供的資料顯示，延布港最近日均原油裝運量已接近峰值，為去年同期的4倍多。
巴基斯坦高度依賴中東油氣進口，其國內能源供應已因美伊戰事受到影響。路透社援引巴方人士的話報道：“戰事結束符合各方最大利益。”（新華社）

### 2026-07-17 17:04:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Current Account #Importance 1

Bulgaria Current Account Deficit Widens in May — Bulgaria’s current account deficit widened sharply to EUR 597.8 million in May 2026 from EUR 88.2 million in the corresponding month of the previous year. The goods account deficit increased to EUR 782.6 million from EUR 419.2 million, while the services account surplus narrowed to EUR 556.9 million from EUR 634.1 million, mainly due to weaker manufacturing services, which stagnated (vs EUR 10.4 million), and transportation services (EUR 190.5 million vs EUR 201.2 million). Meanwhile, the primary income deficit widened slightly to EUR 480.1 million from EUR 479.4 million a year earlier. The secondary income surplus also declined by EUR 68.3 million to EUR 108 million.

Hong Kong Business Morale Improves Slightly in Q3 — Business confidence in Hong Kong edged up to -4 in the third quarter of 2026 from -5 in the previous quarter. The share of respondents expecting a better business condition declined to 10% from 11% in Q2 2026, while those anticipating worse conditions eased slightly to 14% from 16%. Confidence strengthened in manufacturing (5 vs 0 in Q2) and sentiment also improved, though remained negative, in accommodation and food services (-9 vs -35), construction (-6 vs -21), and transportation, storage and courier services (-15 vs -24), information and communications (-7 vs -16), and retail (-2 vs -3). Confidence in import/export trade and wholesale was unchanged at -9. However, optimism eased in financing and insurance (10 vs 12), while sentiment deteriorated sharply in real estate (-11 vs 9) and professional and business services (-10 vs 2).

### 2026-07-17 16:53:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hong Kong #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Hong Kong Unemployment Rate Steady — Hong Kong’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate held steady at 3.7% in the three months ending June 2026, remaining at its lowest level since the period ending August 2025. The number of unemployed people declined by 1,400 month-on-month to 139,700, while total employment increased by 6,800 to 3.6 million. However, labor market conditions among younger workers weakened, with the unemployment rate for those aged 20 to 29 rising to 7.5% from 7%, the highest since the three months ending November 2025. The underemployment rate also edged up to 1.6% from 1.5%. Meanwhile, the labor force participation rate inched higher to 56.2% from 56.1% in the previous period.

### 2026-07-17 16:52:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kazakhstan #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Kazakhstan Industrial Activity Expands — Kazakhstan’s industrial production rose 8.2% year-on-year in June 2026, accelerating from 4.7% in the previous month. The largest jump was recorded in the manufacturing sector, surging 13.7% from 5.2% in the previous month, followed by the supply of electricity, gas, steam, hot water and air conditioning, edging up to 16.9% from 16%. Meanwhile, mining quarrying output slowed to 1.8% from 3%, largely due to a decrease in production of crude oil and natural gas. Activity in water supply, water drainage, waste collection, treatment and disposal, pollution abatement services contracted 1.3% after a 2.9% increase in the previous month. For the first half of the year, industrial production rose 3.5% compared to the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-07-17 16:42:43  #國際

【以色列議會解散 內塔尼亞胡政府轉為看守政府】以色列議會於17日凌晨解散，新一屆議會選舉將在10月27日舉行。以色列總理內塔尼亞胡領導的政府在新政府成立前將作為看守政府繼續履職。根據以色列法律，議會選舉原則上每4年舉行一次。以色列媒體報道說，此次選舉是以色列38年來首次按法定任期而非提前舉行的議會選舉。根據規定，從議會解散起至新政府成立前，現政府將轉為看守政府，原則上不得推動實施重大政策或具有爭議性的立法，僅可處理獲得廣泛支援的法案、日常行政事務及國家安全所需事項。以色列媒體近期公佈的多項民調顯示，內塔尼亞胡領導的執政聯盟預計將在擁有120個席位的議會中獲得約50席，低於組建政府所需的61席，按目前聯盟格局難以繼續執政。（新華社）

Up to 20 mln yuan in support for breakthroughs in core technologies including industrial vertical large models, AI coding models, physical AI, industrial agents, industrial software and industrial internet — with specific aims such as knowledge-graph fusion and text-to-3D parts design. To lower intelligent-input costs, the city encourages industrial cloud platforms to offer low-code agent development, free trials, token experience vouchers and preferential compute plans; subsidies of up to 40 mln yuan for leasing non-affiliated compute resources for industrial model/agent R&D. 【US strikes destroy five Hormozgan bridges, severing Bandar Abbas logistics】US strikes destroyed five bridges in Hormozgan province, cutting road links between Bandar Abbas and surrounding districts. Bandar Abbas, Iran’s primary southern naval logistics hub, supports IRGC, coastal defense, missile, engineering units and transport of military supplies. Damage to transport nodes may delay fuel, ammunition, engineering gear, spare parts and other military materiel, lowering logistics efficiency and degrading operational execution. Rapid deployment and mobility of anti-ship cruise missiles, coastal defense systems, ballistic missile launchers and UAVs could be impaired; with fewer road corridors formerly dispersed assets may become easier to detect and track by ISR. The Hormozgan road network also underpins Iran’s monitoring and reinforcement of Strait of Hormuz coastal bases; damaged routes will hinder troop and resupply movements, radar sustainment, launcher repairs and the maintenance of deterrent/threat capabilities against maritime traffic.

### 2026-07-17 16:36:27  #宏觀 #公司

【電池消費稅政策將分步調整】記者7月17日從財政部瞭解到，財政部、海關總署、稅務總局近日釋出公告，明確自2026年9月1日起，分步調整部分電池產品消費稅政策。我國當前對電池徵收4%的消費稅，這是從2015年2月起開始實施的稅收政策。政策同時明確，對包括鋰原電池、鋰離子蓄電池、太陽能電池在內的七類電池免徵消費稅。根據公告，此次政策調整將分類分步對部分電池恢復徵收消費稅。自2026年9月1日起，對無汞原電池、金屬氫化物鎳蓄電池、鋰原電池、鋰離子蓄電池、全釩液流電池按照2%稅率徵收消費稅；自2027年9月1日起，按照4%稅率徵收消費稅。自2027年4月1日起，對光伏電池（又稱“太陽能電池”）按照2%稅率徵收消費稅；自2028年4月1日起，按照4%稅率徵收消費稅。同時，部分新技術電池在一定期限內享受消費稅免稅優惠。根據公告，自2026年9月1日起至2028年12月31日，對鈉離子電池、固態電池、燃料電池及光伏電池中的鈣鈦礦電池、疊層電池、砷化鎵電池免徵消費稅。業內人士稱，總的來看，此次政策調整順應我國電池產業發展形勢變化，有利於更好發揮消費稅調節作用，促進資源節約和環境保護；有利於促進電池行業健康高質量發展，推動行業技術進步和產業升級。（新華社）

【財政部等部門：調整部分電池消費稅政策】為進一步完善消費稅制度，現將電池消費稅政策調整事項公告如下：一、自2026年9月1日起，對無汞原電池、金屬氫化物鎳蓄電池（又稱氫鎳蓄電池或鎳氫蓄電池）、鋰原電池、鋰離子蓄電池、全釩液流電池按照2%稅率徵收消費稅；自2027年9月1日起，對上述電池產品按照4%稅率徵收消費稅。二、自2027年4月1日起，對光伏電池（又稱太陽能電池）按照2%稅率徵收消費稅；自2028年4月1日起，對光伏電池按照4%稅率徵收消費稅。三、自2026年9月1日起至2028年12月31日，對鈉離子電池、固態電池、燃料電池及光伏電池中的鈣鈦礦電池、疊層電池、砷化鎵電池免徵消費稅。四、納稅人生產、委託加工享受上述減免稅政策的電池產品，應當符合該產品的國家標準，對不符合國家標準或沒有國家標準的產品，不得享受上述減免稅政策。

### 2026-07-17 16:24:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Current Account #Importance 2

Eurozone Current Account Deficit Narrows in May — The Eurozone recorded a current account deficit of €6.19 billion in May 2026, narrowing slightly from a €6.57 billion shortfall a year earlier. The improvement was driven by a sharp reduction in the primary income deficit to €22.44 billion from a record €42.35 billion in May 2025. Meanwhile, the secondary income deficit widened to €13.19 billion from €10.84 billion. The goods surplus narrowed to €9.45 billion, its smallest since April 2023, while the services surplus increased to €19.99 billion from €16.42 billion. For the January–May period, the current account surplus rose to €67.5 billion from €64.37 billion a year earlier.

Nasdaq-100 futures were down more than 2% and S&P 500 futures fell over 1%. Nvidia (NVDA.O) led the "seven megacaps" lower in premarket trading; the Philadelphia Semiconductor Index is approaching bear-market territory and extended Thursday's losses. BARCLAYS strategist Venu Krishna said AI-capex enthusiasm is cooling, but semiconductors still materially outperform the broader market while software lags, implying recent rotation is gradual rather than decisive.

### 2026-07-17 16:13:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Current Account #Importance 1

Italy Current Account Surplus Shrinks in May — Italy’s current account surplus shrank to EUR 593.9 million in May 2026 from EUR 1,906.9 million in the same month of the previous year. This marked the smallest surplus since a deficit in January, as the goods surplus declined to EUR 5,224.4 million from EUR 6,171.5 million, while the services balance swung to a shortfall of EUR 118.2 million from a surplus of EUR 173.9 million a year earlier. The primary income deficit also widened to EUR 4,034.4 million from EUR 3,746.7 million. Meanwhile, the secondary income gap narrowed to EUR 477.9 million from EUR 691.8 million.

【Euro area current account surplus widens in May as primary income rise offsets narrower trade surplus】ECB data on Friday showed the euro area seasonally adjusted current account surplus widened to €25.1 bln in May from €17.5 bln in April, driven by higher primary income (interest, profits and dividends) that offset a narrower trade surplus. Unadjusted data showed a €6.2 bln current account deficit in May versus a €16.7 bln surplus in April. Over the 12 months to May the current account surplus was 1.7% of GDP, down from 2.0% a year earlier.

DAX Down for 3rd Day — The DAX 40 fell 0.5% to around 24,800 on Friday, marking the third straight of losses, pressured by a sharp selloff in tech stocks and concerns about escalating tensions in the Middle East. Oil prices remained elevated as the United States and Iran intensified attacks across the Gulf, disrupting oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz and keeping inflation concerns alive. Markets largely expect the ECB to maintain rates steady next week before potentially delivering a cut in September. Chipmaker Infineon Technologies (-5%) and AI infrastructure-linked Siemens Energy (-2.6%) and Hochtief (-2.5%) were among the worst performers. Deutsche Bank and Commerzbank also faced pressure, falling 1.5% and 1.4%, respectively. On the upside, Deutsche Telekom (2.5%), E.ON (1.7%) and SAP (1.6%) stood out. For the week, the index was set to decline over 1%.

FTSE MIB Falls for Third Session — The FTSE MIB fell to around 52,100 on Friday, extending losses for a third straight session as renewed concerns over elevated AI-related valuations and ongoing tensions between the US and Iran weighed on risk sentiment. Semiconductor firm STMicroelectronics plunged more than 6%, while cable maker Prysmian dropped over 4%, tracking weakness in global technology stocks. Losses also spread across other sectors, with luxury names Moncler and Brunello Cucinelli falling 1.3% and 1.1%, respectively. Financials also weighed on the index, led by heavyweight UniCredit (-1.1%), followed by BPER Banca (-0.9%) and Intesa Sanpaolo (-0.5%). Limiting the downside, utilities outperformed, with Terna (+2.3%), Snam (+2.2%), and Enel (+1.7%) posting solid gains, while Eni rose 1.3% as firmer oil prices supported energy shares. For the week, the index was on track for a second consecutive decline.

【多隊球衣熱銷斷貨 耐克庫存不足錯失世界盃商機】耐克偏偏在美國男足世界盃球衣最該補貨熱銷的關鍵時期庫存告急。如今雖然完成補貨，卻已然錯過了球隊本屆世界盃征程帶來的銷售視窗期。 美國男足在小組賽前兩場接連大勝巴拉圭與澳大利亞，開賽僅兩週，多款美國隊球衣便全部售罄，這足以說明作為球隊贊助商的耐克嚴重低估了這款球衣的市場需求。 耐克緊急加急備貨、向線下門店補發貨品，但補貨送達為時已晚。在美國隊爆冷不敵比利時、早早止步世界盃一週多之後，耐克才於本週上架補庫球衣，上架初期還打出17%的折扣；不過到週四，耐克官網就已經撤下了該優惠。 缺貨問題不只是出在美國隊球衣上。耐克一共贊助16支國家隊，其中至少4支隊伍的球衣全部賣斷。巴西隊部分球衣在小組賽階段就已售空，法國隊正版球員版球衣更是在淘汰賽開始前便無貨可售。古根海姆證券零售業分析師西緬·西格爾認為，球衣斷貨只是耐克整體經營現狀的縮影。這類本該全價熱銷創收卻錯失視窗期的問題，根源在於公司內部多條業務線仍在不斷處理各類經營漏洞、被動補救問題。

【S&P: LNG exports to add $1.4 trillion to US GDP by 2040】S&P Global Energy says US LNG could become the country's second-largest net export sector within five years and add about $1.4 trillion to US GDP by 2040. The study expects the US to be the first country to top 100 million metric tons of LNG exports in a single year in 2025 and projects more than $1 trillion of LNG supply-chain investment by 2040. Following last year’s lift of the LNG export pause, S&P forecasts LNG export-driven outcomes of roughly $2.9 trillion in revenue, $206 billion in tax receipts and about $630 billion in labor income. It also expects US household average gas bills to rise about 1.6% in 2026–31 and says export growth will roughly double gas supply demand to about 36 bcf/d by 2031.

### 2026-07-17 15:49:51  #MKT News #SPCX.O #Impact bearish

【SpaceX falls below IPO price within a month; could drop another 30% by 2028 — three reasons】SpaceX (SPCX.O) completed the largest IPO last month, briefly rallied, then slid below its $135 IPO price. The report argues the stock could fall another 30% by 2028. First, IPO-driven FOMO is fading: shares have fallen about 40% from a roughly $225 peak as investors pivot from narrative to fundamentals. Second, SpaceX’s AI strategy underpins its ~$1.8 tln valuation but is loss-making: management cites an AI TAM of $28.5 tln (AI-related $26.5 tln), yet 2025 AI revenue was $3.2 bln with an operating loss of $6.4 bln, implying continued heavy investment and limited near-term profit contribution. Third, competitive risk is rising after China and Japan completed tests of reusable rockets, narrowing SpaceX’s technological lead even if rivals are not targeting US government contracts. Conclusion: fundamentals remain solid, but a $1.8 tln market cap looks aggressive; a 30% decline to roughly $1.26 tln by 2028 (about 18 months) would leave valuation better aligned with execution risk.

### 2026-07-17 15:41:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Stock Market #Importance 1

European Stocks Lower as Chip Sell-Off Weighs — European stocks were lower on Friday, with the STOXX 50 falling 0.8% and the STOXX 600 declining 0.6%, as a sharp sell-off in chip shares spread from the US to Asia-Pacific markets and weighed heavily on the tech sector. Renewed concerns over stretched valuations overshadowed strong earnings from ASML and TSMC, while escalating hostilities in the Middle East further dampened risk appetite. Tech stocks led the declines, with ASML Holding (-3.9%), Infineon (-4.8%) and STMicroelectronics (-5.8%) among the biggest losers. Burberry also underperformed, dropping around 5% after results failed to impress investors. In contrast, utilities and consumer staples posted gains. Saab rose 2.6% after reporting record orders. Vodafone advanced 3.0% and National Grid gained 2.2%, ranking among the session's best performers. On the week, the STOXX 50 is up 0.2% while the STOXX 600 added 0.4% so far.

### 2026-07-17 15:39:20  #宏觀 #國際

【中方如何看待成立世界人工智慧合作組織的意義？外交部答澎湃】7月17日，外交部發言人林劍主持例行記者會。

澎湃新聞（www.thepaper.cn）記者提問，昨天，成立世界人工智慧合作組織協定簽署儀式在上海舉行。中方如何看待成立該組織的意義和影響？該組織同聯合國框架下的人工智慧治理平臺是什麼關係？

林劍介紹，7月16日，成立世界人工智慧合作組織協定的簽署儀式在上海舉行。中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅出席並代表中國政府簽署協定。哈薩克、寮國、巴基斯坦、俄羅斯、印度尼西亞等29個國家代表簽署協定，成為創始成員國。聯合國秘書長古特雷斯等國家和國際組織代表出席儀式。

林劍表示，中方始終積極踐行習近平主席提出的《全球人工智慧治理倡議》，秉持以人為本、向上向善理念，推動人工智慧全球治理。

正如習近平主席今天上午在2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議開幕式上指出的，世界人工智慧合作組織在上海應運而生，這是中方響應全球南方呼聲，團結國際社會，積極推動人工智慧發展和治理的重大舉措，將成為人工智慧發展史上的一個重要里程碑。

林劍指出，中方一貫主張在聯合國框架下推進人工智慧全球治理，推動聯合國大會協商一致透過人工智慧能力建設國際合作決議，支援落實全球數字契約和聯合國可持續發展目標。下階段，中方將會同各創始成員國推動組織儘早啟動運轉。

林劍表示，倡議成立世界人工智慧合作組織是中方推動構建人類命運共同體，落實全球治理倡議的又一生動實踐。中方願同各方一道，遵循聯合國憲章宗旨，秉持共商、共建、共享理念，堅持以人為本原則，加強人工智慧發展戰略、治理規則、技術標準的對接協調，促進人工智慧國際合作和全球治理，確保人工智慧朝著有益、安全、公平方向健康有序發展。

【微信公眾平臺運營中心：停車服務類小程式不得使用誤導類廣告干擾核心操作】7月16日晚，微信公眾平臺運營中心釋出關於進一步規範停車服務類小程式運營的公告。公告稱，近期，平臺發現部分停車服務類小程式在服務過程中存在不規範運營行為。停車服務類小程式不得在使用者使用服務過程中強制、誘導或變相獲取使用者手機號。使用者使用停車繳費服務後，小程式應優先展示訂單狀態及支付按鈕，不得透過彈窗、浮層、遮罩層、全屏廣告、半屏廣告等形式遮擋頁面內容或阻斷使用者操作，不得使用誤導類廣告干擾核心操作。為保障使用者體驗，維護小程式生態健康，平臺將針對以上違規行為開展專項治理。

### 2026-07-17 15:35:34  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #Magnificent 7 #Star Stocks #NVIDIA

【Kimi saves on compute but not hardware — where are the costs?】Kimi K3 contains 2.8 tln parameters, routing each token to 16 of 896 experts and using MXFP4 weights with MXFP8 activations. The sparse-expert design cuts actual compute and low-precision weights reduce storage, but model scale still creates heavy VRAM demand. A single DGX B200 offers 1.44 TB of GPU memory—roughly comparable to the baseline volume of K3’s 4‑bit weights—but once caches, activations and runtime overhead are included it is unlikely to host the full model. B300 and MI355X single‑GPU configurations provide 288 GB VRAM and are better suited for large-model deployment. The bigger bottleneck is interconnect: multi‑B200 clusters can jointly hold the model but frequent cross‑GPU expert exchanges force heavy cross‑server traffic, which is materially less efficient than GB300 NVL72’s internal NVLink. Kimi therefore recommends supernodes of 64+ accelerators; high‑density deployments also require high‑speed interconnect, parallel software stacks, and upgraded power and cooling. In short: reduced compute, high memory footprint, and heavy interconnect requirements.

India 10Y Yield Steadies Ahead of Debt Sale — The yields on India’s 10-Year G-Sec hovered around 6.75%, steadying after falling in the previous session as investors awaited the auction of INR 320 billion worth of three- and 30-year government bonds. Yields found support from relatively stable crude oil prices near $85 per barrel and softer US Treasury yields, which eased external pressure despite renewed geopolitical tensions in the Middle East. Demand for Indian sovereign debt also remained resilient despite higher-than-expected domestic inflation, rupee weakness, and escalating geopolitical tensions. Overseas investors purchased about INR 16.5 billion of bonds under the Fully Accessible Route this week amid expectations of India's inclusion in the Bloomberg bond index, while foreign banks turned net buyers on Thursday, acquiring more than INR 50 billion of government bonds after a three-session selling streak, supporting sentiment ahead of the debt sale.

UK Gas Prices Continue to Rise — UK natural gas prices rose toward 134 pence per therm on Friday, bringing total gains this week to more than 14%, amid increasing threats to Persian Gulf supplies as US-Iran attacks intensified. The US carried out fresh strikes on Iran on Thursday, following previous attacks that struck an unladen oil tanker bound for Iranian ports after the reimposition of a blockade. Tehran has countered with attacks targeting US military bases in neighboring countries and has reportedly called Yemen’s Houthi rebel group to shut the Red Sea export route if Washington targets Iranian power infrastructure. UK gas prices climbed to their highest in over three months and have surged over 28% in July so far, erasing an over 18% decline in Q2, as supply disruptions and stronger power generation demand constrain Europe’s ability to rebuild inventories before winter. Adding to this, Qatar, the Middle East's largest LNG exporter, halted plans to boost output after one of its tankers was attacked near Hormuz.

### 2026-07-17 15:20:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Czechia Producer Prices Rise Less Than Expected — Producer prices in the Czech Republic rose 1.3% year-on-year in June 2026, below market expectations of 1.5% and easing from 1.5% in the previous month. This marked the third consecutive month of annual growth after fourteen months of contraction. Price growth slowed for manufactured products (2.8% vs. 3.6% in May), weighed down by steeper declines in food products (-5.0% vs. -4.0%), softer declines in motor vehicles, trailers, and semi-trailers (-1.4% vs. -1.5%), and slower growth in machinery and equipment (0.7% vs. 0.9%). Cost increases also eased for mining and quarrying (2.1% vs. 2.3%). Meanwhile, deflation softened for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning (-4.4% vs. -6.0%), while inflation for water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation activities was unchanged at 3.9%. On a monthly basis, producer prices fell 0.4% in June, deepening from a 0.1% decline in May and marking the second consecutive month of contraction after three months of inflation.

### 2026-07-17 15:18:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Austria Inflation Confirmed at 3-Month Low — The annual inflation rate in Austria slowed to 3.2% in June 2026 from a five-month high of 3.7% in the previous month, confirming initial estimates. This marked the lowest reading since March, as price pressures eased mainly due to a moderation in fuel and heating oil inflation, while food prices also rose at a slower pace. Despite the slowdown, transport remained the largest contributor to inflation, with prices rising 6.5%, driven by fuel prices that were still 16.3% higher than a year earlier and airfares that surged 22.4%. Housing and utilities rose 3.0%, while restaurants and accommodation services increased 4.5%. Health costs climbed 4.4%, whereas food and non-alcoholic beverages rose a modest 1.3%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices were unchanged in June after rising 0.1% in May. The harmonised inflation rate (HICP) was also confirmed at 3.2%.

### 2026-07-17 15:14:49  #Trading Economics #Markets #Taiwan #Stock Market #Importance 1

TAIEX Ends 6.5% Lower on Tech Selloff — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, plunged 2,954 points, or 6.5%, to close at 42,671 on Friday, after being almost flat in the previous session, tracking a decline on Wall Street overnight amid a sharp selloff in semiconductor stocks. The broader index hit its lowest level since May 22, mainly weighed down by electronic technology stocks, which plunged 7.5%. TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker, which accounts for more than 40% of the market's total value, slumped 7.3%, as strong results failed to meet investors' lofty expectations, stoking worries over heavy spending and a weaker outlook for profitability. MediaTek and Delta Electronics plunged 8.9% and 8.7%, respectively, while ASE Technology and United Microelectronics each slumped 10.0%. For the week, the index fell 5.9%, posting its second consecutive weekly loss amid a global tech stock selloff.

### 2026-07-17 15:14:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #Moldova #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Moldova Producer Prices Fall for Second Month — Producer prices in Moldova fell 0.4% year-on-year in June 2026, following a 0.1% drop in the previous month. This marked the second consecutive month of contraction and the lowest reading since December 2024. Price growth moderated in the manufacturing sector (0.6% vs 0.9% in May), driven largely by slower increases in the prices of food products (1.9% vs 2.2%), beverages (1.7% vs 1.8%), rubber and plastic products (0.7% vs 3.4%), and furniture (0.5% vs 0.6%). Additionally, prices for electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply declined by 12.8%, matching the pace recorded in the previous two months. In contrast, costs increased in mining and quarrying (6.8% vs 6.5%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 0.1% in June, easing from a 0.4% increase in each of the previous two months.

【Strategists Turn More Bullish on European Stocks; UBS Sees STOXX Europe 600 Up 8% by Year-end】A July survey of 18 strategists shows rising optimism on European equities as earnings revisions support upside. UBS was most bullish, projecting the STOXX Europe 600 to climb about 8% by year-end. BofA, Deutsche Bank and Kepler Cheuvreux also raised targets. The surveyed strategists’ average expects the index at 647 by end-2026 — under 1% above current levels — but bearish views have narrowed: only five respondents expect a decline. UBS strategist Gerry Fowler said upside risks now outweigh downside risks. CITIGROUP’s head of European equity strategy Beata Manthey said she remains constructive over the next 12 months and is encouraged by the magnitude, breadth and timing of recent upward revisions to corporate earnings.

【《功夫女足》被爆遭“偷票房”，電影院回應手寫票空白票：因票紙潮溼沒打上墨】近日，有網友反映觀看電影《功夫女足》時，收到手寫電影票，質疑有電影院以手寫票、空白票、不透過電影發行系統出機打票等方式“偷票房”。7月16日晚，周星馳在社交媒體上分享了一組《功夫女足》手寫票的圖片，每張圖片均配上了3個問號。福建漳州一位網友吐槽，他在櫃檯買票時，打出來的票是空白的，朋友在網上買的票則是正常的。7月16日，電影院向有關媒體解釋，當天才換的一卷票紙太潮溼，導致油墨顯示不清，票是從系統中出的《功夫女足》正規電影票，並沒有“偷票房”。涉事電影院工作人員稱，相關員工是新培訓的，所以她沒有注意到這一點，“（現在已經）把潮溼的票紙換掉了。因為這個事情，我們那名員工已經被停職了。”偷票房是怎麼回事？常見套路有這麼幾種：手寫白條收據，人進去了，錢沒進系統；移花接木，買A電影打出B電影的票，B電影白賺票房和人次，A電影分不到錢；更隱蔽的還有收票錢開餐飲發票的“套餐票”，用會員卡扣費後臺截留的“會員票”。（每經）

【國防部：菲方一些人搞政治作秀改變不了有關島礁屬於中國的歷史和法理事實】7月17日下午，國防部新聞發言人蔣斌大校就近期涉軍問題釋出訊息。

記者：據報道，菲律賓防長近日赴中業島參加紀念“南海仲裁裁決”十年活動。請問對此有何評論？

蔣斌：中國對包括中業島在內的南沙群島及其附近海域擁有無可爭辯的主權。菲律賓防長受到中方制裁，妄圖透過大搞政治作秀、煽動民族情緒體現所謂“對華強硬”，撈取政治私利，最終損害的是菲國家和人民的根本利益。“南海仲裁案”從頭到尾都是一場披著法律外衣的政治鬧劇，所謂“裁決”完全不具備任何法律效力。中方不接受、不承認，也不接受任何基於該“裁決”的主張和行動。無論菲方一些人如何表演，都改變不了有關島礁屬於中國的歷史和法理事實，動搖不了中國軍隊維護國家領土主權和海洋權益的堅定決心。

【國防部：希望有關方客觀理性看待中俄軍事合作】7月17日下午，國防部新聞發言人蔣斌大校就近期涉軍問題釋出訊息。

記者：據報道，近期中俄兩國海軍舉行聯合演習及海上聯合巡航。有分析稱，此次聯演恰逢美盟開展系列聯演之際，表明中俄致力於打破美西方主導的世界格局。請問對此有何評論？

蔣斌：近期，中俄“海上聯合-2026”聯合演習位青島附近海空域成功舉行，雙方參演部隊圍繞聯合偵察、防空反導等多個課目展開演練，順利完成了各項任務，目前已轉入海上聯合巡航階段。上述活動是中俄兩軍年度合作計劃內安排，旨在提升兩軍聯合行動能力、共同應對海上安全威脅，與當前國際和地區局勢無關。

需要強調的是，中俄兩軍合作始終堅持不結盟、不對抗、不針對第三方的原則，為捍衛兩國主權安全、維護國際與地區和平穩定發揮了重要作用。我們希望有關方客觀理性看待中俄軍事合作，停止主觀臆測和抹黑炒作。

【特朗普1.5萬億美元國防預算遇阻 共和黨加急撥款600億填補伊朗戰爭缺口】美國眾議院共和黨人為支撐對伊朗戰事推出的最新撥款方案，或將徹底擊碎特朗普敲定1.5萬億美元國防總預算的設想。 中東戰事大量消耗攔截導彈與各類彈藥庫存，給美國國防部賬面留下鉅額資金窟窿。共和黨高層正加急推動一項600億美元專項法案填補缺口。為繞開參議院民主黨方面的阻撓，共和黨打算啟用預算和解程式推進該法案——該特殊立法流程無需湊齊參議院常規所需60票終結冗長辯論，僅需簡單多數即可透過法案。 特朗普政府官員此前曾敦促議員先出臺規模更小的法案，優先滿足戰時緊急資金需求。但這一方案會重創特朗普這份體量驚人的國防預算藍圖：總統原本計劃，1.5萬億總預算中，拿出3500億美元同樣走這套預算和解通道獲批落地。 今年春季國會已經單獨透過一份700億美元和解法案，用於移民管控與邊境巡邏專案撥款。參眾兩院共和黨均僅握有微弱多數席位，在中期選舉臨近的情況下，本年度接連再推出兩份和解法案，在政治層面幾乎沒有可行性。

### 2026-07-17 15:01:54  #其他

【國防部：願與東盟國家一道為共建“五大家園”貢獻智慧力量】7月17日下午，國防部新聞發言人蔣斌大校就近期涉軍問題釋出訊息。 記者：近期第四屆中國—東盟防務智庫交流活動在雲南昆明舉行，請進一步介紹有關情況。 蔣斌：7月8日至10日，第四屆中國—東盟防務智庫交流活動在雲南昆明成功舉辦。本次活動以“踐行亞洲安全模式：中國東盟攜手應對世界變局”為主題，議題涵蓋區域爭端解決的亞洲正規化、海上安全與海洋命運共同體建設、以務實合作增強戰略互信、面向未來的防務安全合作等。來自中國和東盟11國的約150位防務官員、智庫學者出席。各方代表普遍認為，“安危與共、求同存異、對話協商”的亞洲安全模式摒棄零和博弈的傳統安全觀，追求共同安全，對凝聚各國安全共識、維護亞太地區和平穩定具有重要意義；越是國際風雲變幻，地區各國越要把穩合作之舵、同築共贏之路。 今年是中國—東盟建立對話關係35週年，也是雙方建立全面戰略伙伴關係5週年。我們願與東盟國家一道，堅守和平這一最大的共同利益，堅持對話不對抗，結伴不結盟，攜手應對各類安全挑戰，為共建和平、安寧、繁榮、美麗、友好的“五大家園”貢獻智慧與力量。

### 2026-07-17 14:56:49  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Wheat Futures Retreat — Wheat futures slipped to around $6.70 per bushel in mid-July, retreating from a more than two-year high reached on July 15 as traders locked in profits. Despite the pullback, prices remained elevated as the Russia-Ukraine conflict showed no signs of easing, with Russian forces resuming strikes on Ukrainian port infrastructure along the Black Sea coast in response to recent Ukrainian attacks on Russian vessels in the Sea of Azov and the Black Sea. Adding pressure on prices, the USDA reported US wheat net export sales of 235,100 metric tons for the week ended July 9, below market expectations. Meanwhile, supply concerns emerged after the Grain Industry Association of Western Australia projected the state’s 2026 wheat harvest at 9.5 million tons, down nearly 30% from 13.3 million tons in 2025 due to hotter and drier conditions. Australia, one of the world’s largest wheat exporters, supplies key markets across Southeast Asia, the Middle East, and China.

Malaysia Stocks Hover Near 2-Month Peak After Solid Q2 GDP Data — Bursa Malaysia’s KLCI extended gains into Friday afternoon, hovering near 1,729 and edging toward a two-month high. Sentiment was buoyed by upbeat Q2 GDP flash data, with resilient services and electronics exports helping offset the drag from Middle East tensions. For the first half of the year, Malaysia’s economy expanded 5.6% yoy, accelerating from 4.5% in the corresponding period of 2025. Meanwhile, inflation eased a bit in June to 1.9% from May’s 2%, as fuel subsidies cushioned higher crude costs. Financials led the strength, with gains from Malayan Banking (1.6%), Hong Leong Bank (1.5%), and CIMB Group (1.2%). Energy and communications also supported the upside, highlighted by Petronas Chemicals’ 2.8% climb, Telekom Malaysia’s 0.9% rise, and Carlsberg Brewery’s 0.8% gain. With the benchmark tracking a 2.1% advance so far, the market is on course for a third consecutive weekly gain, underscoring steady momentum amid improving macro signals.

Japanese Shares Tumble on Tech Rout — The Nikkei 225 Index plunged 4.03% to close at 64,140 on Friday, while the broader Topix Index fell 2.72% to 3,919, with both benchmarks finishing at their lowest levels in more than a month as a global selloff in semiconductor stocks accelerated amid mounting concerns over heavy AI-related spending and stretched valuations. Investor sentiment was also weighed down by the escalating conflict between the US and Iran, which drove oil prices higher and heightened inflationary and interest rate concerns. Technology and AI-related shares led the declines in Japan, with Kioxia Holdings tumbling 16.1%, Tokyo Electron sliding 8.1%, SoftBank Group losing 9%, Advantest falling 7.2%, and Taiyo Yuden dropping 11.9%. For the week, the Nikkei 225 and Topix indexes recorded losses of 6.4% and 2.9%, respectively.

【SoftBank to sell ¥60bn bonds to Japanese institutions to fund AI investments】SoftBank Group plans to issue about ¥60bn (≈$369m) of bonds to Japanese institutional investors this month, offering roughly ¥50bn of 3-year and ¥10bn of 5-year tranches in the week of July 27, according to lead underwriter Daiwa Securities. The move underscores growing financing needs; SoftBank earlier raised ¥678bn via retail subordinated notes and tapped US and European bond markets in April. Renewed market concern over AI investment returns has weighed on semiconductor-related names including Arm and pressured SoftBank shares, which have slipped from early-June highs after reports an OpenAI IPO may be delayed. SoftBank has been considering using its OpenAI stake as collateral for margin loans and has scaled planned financing down from $10bn to about $6bn.

【Panshi scientific foundation model 2.0 launched with domestically controlled heterogeneous compute stack】Chinese Academy of Sciences institutes unveiled Panshi scientific foundation model 2.0 at the 2026 World AI Conference. It is paired with an integrated research-intelligence platform aggregating more than 8,000 professional scientific tools and a domestically controlled heterogeneous compute stack described as 'supercompute + smartcompute + fastcompute'. ### 2026-07-17 14:43:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #Romania #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Romanian Industrial Output Drops by Most in Over a Year — Industrial production in Romania declined 5.3% year-on-year in May 2026, following a revised 3.0% drop in April and marking the sharpest fall since March 2025. The decline in manufacturing output deepened (-5.5% vs -4.3% in April), amid steeper declines across most subsectors, notably the manufacture of motor vehicles, trailers and semi-trailers (-15.3% vs -14.5%), fabricated metal products, except machinery and equipment (-4.3% vs 1.0%), rubber and plastic products (-4.5% vs 1.0%), chemicals and chemical products (-1.6% vs 0.1%), and furniture (-23.0% vs -16.9%). Output in mining and quarrying also contracted further (-4.8% vs -1.0%), while electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply fell 3.1%, reversing a 3.8% rise in the previous month. On a monthly basis, industrial output decreased 2.8%, after a revised 1.6% increase in April.

【Kioxia rout drags Nikkei as AI rally cools; bottom still unclear】Kioxia Holdings plunged as much as 16% intraday on Friday as investors unwound leverage in AI-related tech names, dragging the Nikkei 225 down more than 6% to a one-month intraday low. Taiwan’s TAIEX fell about 6.5%. South Korea was closed for a holiday; Australia, Hong Kong and Singapore markets also declined. Kioxia, a key beneficiary of this year’s AI-led rally, hit intraday limit down; its market value fell to roughly ¥28 trillion from a peak near ¥60 trillion (about $370 billion), and the stock is about 50% below its June 22 intraday high. JP Morgan’s quantitative strategies team said recent market sentiment has swung toward excess pessimism — narratives that the AI boom is ending or an AI bubble is bursting — and added: “Although supply-demand adjustment in AI names may be nearing completion, AI stocks driven by sentiment and flows may still need more time to bottom as these pessimistic narratives fully unwind.”

ASX Slips 0.3% This Week — Australia's ASX 200 fell 58 points, or 0.7%, to close at 8,783 on Friday, pressured by sharp losses in U.S. stock futures after chip stocks tumbled on Wall Street Thursday following their blistering rally earlier this year. Escalating Middle East tensions and weaker shipping flows through the Strait of Hormuz added to the cautious tone. The local benchmark reversed from a muted prior session, ending the week down 0.3% and marking a second straight weekly decline. Traders cautiously braced for Australia's labor data next week after May logged the strongest jobs growth in five months, even as unemployment stayed near a four-year top. Tech, non-energy minerals, manufacturing, and consumer non-durables led falls. Miners dropped 2.9%, with BHP and Rio Tinto sliding 2.9% and 2.5%, while gold names Evolution Mining (-4.2%) and Northern Star (-4.0%) also weighed. The big four banks eased between 0.2% and 1.1%. Energy stocks bucked the trend, lifted by Woodside Energy (2.9%) and Santos (1.7%).

【Both sides face high costs from a protracted war; Iran unlikely to accept ceasefire without security guarantees】An Australian National University professor said Iran treats the conflict as existential and, despite economic strain and diplomatic pressure, is unlikely to accept a ceasefire unless it secures explicit guarantees for its safety. He noted Iran’s long experience under sanctions and domestic production capacity, and said Tehran is unlikely to show weakness or yield even after military attrition. U.S. media report Iran rapidly resumed drone production after the April ceasefire; some analysts say Tehran could replenish drone inventories within months. An International Crisis Group security expert warned the rapid consumption of key U.S. munitions, including Patriot interceptors and Tomahawk cruise missiles, is striking and raises concern for future contingencies with other great powers. Both sides therefore face pressure points that make a prolonged war costly, though the ANU professor said the U.S. is suffering greater reputational damage as global observers see limits to its ability to resolve the Iran issue.

【上海鋼聯：預計下半年鋼材價格整體以區間震盪為主】7月17日，2026“我的鋼鐵”年中大會暨鋼鐵產業鏈協同創新高質量發展論壇在北京召開。上海鋼聯預計，今年下半年鋼材價格整體以區間震盪為主，難以出現單邊趨勢性行情，價格高低波動區間與上半年基本特平。地緣局勢方面，美伊談判持續進行中，但市場對此敏感度降低，預計油價波動預計逐漸趨向合理，上半年因地緣衝突帶來的通脹溢價與能源利多消退。原料端，國內煤礦安全整改工作逐漸收尾，焦煤產量有序釋放，原料供給緊張格局逐漸緩解，煤焦價格或衝高回落，但價格重心明顯高於上半年；鐵礦石供應保持寬鬆，價格走低；總體成本端相對穩定。鋼材供需方面，下半年鋼材終端需求延續溫和修復態勢，基建特續託底市場，製造業需求穩步回暖。同時鋼廠將根據市場行情靈活調整生產節奏，延續供需弱平衡態勢，難以出現供需大幅錯配的情況。整體來看，下半年鋼材市場多空力量均衡，缺乏驅動價格大幅變化的核心變數。

### 2026-07-17 13:53:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Rebounds, but Posts Weekly Loss — The NZX 50 climbed 80 points, or 0.6%, to 13,695 on Friday, reversing its morning weakness, snapping a four-session losing streak, and rebounding from its lowest level since July 2, reached a day earlier, mainly boosted by gains in the energy, materials, and consumer discretionary sectors. Fresh data also lifted sentiment, as food inflation eased to 2.5% in June from 3.2% in May, marking the softest increase in 16 months. However, persistently high oil prices limited the gains, as inflation concerns fueled expectations of further interest rate hikes. Traders remained cautious ahead of the release of Q2 inflation data due next Tuesday amid elevated oil prices. RBNZ Chief Economist Paul Conway recently warned that sticky inflation could prompt further interest rate hikes. Among the top performers were Freightways Group (1.5%), Meridian Energy (1.4%), South Port New Zealand (1.4%), and Chorus (1.2%). For the week, the index fell 0.6%, after rising in the previous week.

Palm Oil Eases, Still Set for Weekly Gain — Malaysian palm oil futures edged lower, hovering below MYR 4,600 per tonne after recent gains amid softer edible oil prices on the Dalian markets. Demand concerns also pressured risk appetite, as palm oil imports by top buyer India fell to a 14-month low in June after a narrower discount to rival oils curbed purchases. Meanwhile, China's economy grew at its slowest pace since late 2022 in Q2, raising concerns over demand from another key importer. Despite the pullback, the contract remained on track for its first weekly gain in four, up about 1.7%, lifted by improving export prospects. Cargo surveyors noted palm oil shipments during July 1-15 rose between 4% and 12.4% from the same period in June. Meanwhile, Malaysia raised its August crude palm oil reference price, keeping the export duty at 10%. Crude oil prices also stayed elevated amid limited flows through the Strait of Hormuz as the U.S. and Iran intensified attacks, lending support to palm oil through stronger biodiesel demand.

【China issues International AI Ethics Governance Action Plan】At the 2026 World Artificial Intelligence Conference in Shanghai, the Ministry of Industry and Information Technology (MIIT), with other agencies, issued the International AI Ethics Governance Action Plan. Described as a public good for the international community, the plan offers a principled, operational framework for global AI governance under the UN Future Pact and its annex, the Global Digital Compact. It targets AI lifecycle ethical governance, risk classification and tiered prevention and control, construction of agile governance mechanisms, industry-chain coordination and ecosystem development, and cultivation of social norms, and calls on countries to act according to national conditions and to strengthen policy coordination and practical cooperation on a consult-build-share basis.

【Japan ruling party proposes tighter shareholder rights, raises threshold for extraordinary meetings】A Liberal Democratic Party panel proposed raising the threshold for shareholders to requisition an extraordinary general meeting from 3% of voting rights to at least 5%, and tightening eligibility to submit shareholder proposals to holders of at least 1% of voting rights (current threshold: 300 votes). The panel also recommends excluding business-execution matters from items shareholders may seek to amend in articles of incorporation. It plans to push the measures into law in the fiscal year beginning April 2027. The proposals follow calls from METI and business lobby Keidanren for limits on shareholder rights and have drawn criticism from activist investors.

【Dolat gives NSE 'sell' ahead of planned IPO, says tighter derivatives rules will curb volumes and market share】Dolat Capital Market Pvt. has assigned a 'sell' rating to National Stock Exchange of India Ltd (NSE) as the bourse prepares what would be India’s largest IPO. NSE operates the world’s largest derivatives market by volume. Dolat warns that tighter regulation of Indian equity derivatives will depress trading volumes and reduce NSE’s market share, and says current high equity valuations limit upside. Recent regulatory moves cited include increases in contract size and a rule restricting weekly-expiring option contracts to one benchmark index per exchange. NSE has filed for an IPO of about $3.0bn and plans to list in September subject to SEBI approval.

### 2026-07-17 13:15:29  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #SSE Composite #Impact bearish #SZSE Component #Hang Seng Index

【Valuation reset hits memory chain; why A-shares faced panic liquidity resonance】On July 16 afternoon China A-shares and Hong Kong-listed tech plunged. The Sci‑Tech Innovation 50 Index crashed and the ChiNext Index fell over 5%. The rout reflected three converging negatives: global semiconductor de‑leveraging, a wave of overseas geo‑legal/regulatory pressure, and a transmission of frothy primary‑market valuations into the secondary market that triggered a liquidity stampede. In a sensitive window funds executed indiscriminate risk‑off trades, hitting concentrated hard‑tech indexes hardest. Run‑like collapses in high‑beta hard‑tech and AI compute names pushed profitable long positions at quant/private and multi‑strategy funds into redemption and risk‑limit stress; forced cross‑sector liquidations to raise cash drained liquidity from previously low‑priced sectors and amplified afternoon losses in CHINA'S SHANGHAI COMPOSITE INDEX and the Shenzhen Composite Index. Downside momentum is mainly from forced deleveraging of levered longs and passive de‑risking by mutual funds. Until the selloff in major overseas semiconductor names stabilizes and China memory‑chain valuation baselines are reformed, the tech sector faces a short‑term clearing of positions.

### 2026-07-17 12:51:52  #公司

【樂事因藍色薯片道歉】日前有消費者稱在樂事薯片中發現了一塊“藍色薯片”，此事件持續發酵。 16日，該消費者在社交媒體平臺發帖稱，已經收到樂事方面發來的情況說明，樂事方面致歉並表示：經核實，藍色薯片為工廠使用的著色驗證薯片，不影響食品安全與品質。17日，樂事方面向記者確認，品牌方已把調查結果反饋給消費者。 報道稱，消費者在發現藍色薯片後與樂事方面進行了溝通。消費者透露，百事食品消費者質量熱線客服來電跟進本次藍色薯片問題，並表達了歉意。經過溝通，消費者在確認能自行保留一半正常黃色薯片、一半藍色異常薯片作為留存的情況下，快遞寄回疑似出現問題的薯片。 樂事方面在最新情況說明中提到，經核實，異物為工廠使用的著色驗證薯片，用於確認裝置和流程的穩定性，是食品生產過程中常用的工藝驗證手段之一。 說明稱，工廠使用的著色劑符合GB2760《食品安全國家標準 食品新增劑使用標準》相關要求，不影響食品安全與品質。與此同時，樂事方面也表達了歉意。 “工廠主動向我發出活動邀請，參觀工廠及生產流水線，讓我實地瞭解生產工藝和品控措施，讓紙質性的說明更具透明性和說服力。”消費者稱，目前兩項訴求都已得到解決：一是獲得情況說明，二是該袋薯片的退款。 公開資訊顯示，樂事品牌歸屬於百事公司，於1993年進入中國市場。（中國新聞網）

【回撥是佈局良機？創業板50ETF華安（159949）成交22.62億領跑同類 機構：黃金買點】7月17日，市場早盤集體調整，三大指數低開低走，創指跌超4％。受行情拖累，創業板50ETF華安（159949）早盤下跌4.92％，報1.681元，換手率11.38％，成交額22.62億元，居同類標的ETF首位。華寶基金認為，從長期來看，本輪科技行情整體大頂或尚未出現，自主可控板塊的降溫更多是出於市場降低波動率的考慮。近期自主可控板塊不排除反抽後仍存在部分止盈壓力，但經過震盪消化後，市場對於科技主線尤其是自主可控方向的長期邏輯尚未發生變化，三、四季度仍有較多產業催化因素，整體向下風險相對有限。東方財富策略分析師陳果認為，當前正迎來年內難得的“黃金買點”，主要指數有望在下半年創出階段新高。其核心理由包括：二季度GDP（4.3％）為經濟增速底，下半年增速預計將改善；二季度A股微觀資金面為資金底，下半年同樣有望改善；情緒指標顯示近期市場情緒已接近低位；下半年大批“老資產”處於盈利復甦初期，估值仍處低位；下半年多家中國優質科技公司登陸A股，有望吸引增量資金流入，提升市場活躍度與風險偏好。上述五大條件均優於上半年，中國股市有望較快企穩並震盪上行。配置上，繼續看好非銀金融、新舊能源、網際網路、醫藥、消費以及國產AI產業鏈中的真科技龍頭。投資者可直接透過股票賬戶交易創業板50ETF華安（159949），也可透過聯接基金（A類：160422；C類：160424；I類：022654；Y類：022976）參與。

### 2026-07-17 12:48:56  #宏觀

【習近平出席2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議開幕式並發表主旨講話】7月17日上午，國家主席習近平在上海世界會客廳出席2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議開幕式並發表主旨講話。
黃浦江畔，潮啟新瀾。世界會客廳內嘉賓雲集。
習近平同與會的國家元首、政府首腦、國際組織負責人及各國代表團團長集體合影。
在熱烈的掌聲中，習近平發表題為《攜手構建公正合理的全球人工智慧治理體系》的主旨講話。
習近平指出，當前，世界百年變局加速演進，新一輪科技革命和產業變革加速突破，全球人工智慧技術創新進入前所未有的活躍期，既蘊含巨大機遇，也面臨治理挑戰。人類不得不直面時代之問：當機器開始思考，人類如何與之相處？當演算法參與決策，安全如何保障？當技術挑戰倫理，治理如何跟上？當鴻溝不斷拉大，普惠如何實現？中方認為，各國應當秉持以人為本、向上向善理念，讓人工智慧成為促進共同繁榮、維護共同安全的一個重要動力源，攜手構建公正合理的全球人工智慧治理體系。
習近平就此提出4點意見：
第一，堅持開放共贏，驅動創新發展。抓住難得的歷史性機遇，鼓勵開源開放、合作共享，全面促進人工智慧科技創新、產業發展、場景應用，協同推進傳統產業改造升級、新興產業培育壯大、未來產業前瞻佈局，讓人工智慧賦能千行百業。
第二，強化風險意識，確保安全可控。高度重視人工智慧引發的各類內生和衍生風險，推動構建法律法規、技術監測、風險預警、應急響應體系，築牢安全底線，防範濫用惡用，確保人工智慧始終處於人類控制之下。共同反對在人工智慧領域泛化國家安全概念、把本國安全凌駕於他國安全之上的做法。
第三，鼓勵包容並蓄，促進文明互鑑。用全人類共同價值塑造人工智慧的價值觀，善用人工智慧技術增進不同文明的理解和包容，促進不同文明交流互鑑，精心培育各美其美、美美與共的文明百花園。
第四，倡導和衷共濟，完善全球治理。踐行真正的多邊主義，切實發揮聯合國的重要作用，加強人工智慧發展戰略、治理規則、技術標準的對接協調，早日形成具有廣泛共識的全球治理框架，讓這一前沿技術更好造福人類社會。幫助全球南方國家加強能力建設，彌合數智鴻溝，促進可持續發展，避免在人工智慧領域造成新的歷史不公。（新華社）

【NDRC unveils AI cooperation action plan and international casebook at World AI Conference】At the 2026 World Artificial Intelligence Conference in Shanghai, the National Development and Reform Commission (NDRC) and other agencies released two outputs: the Artificial Intelligence Cooperation Development Action Plan and the China Smart·Benefits the World (2026) casebook. The Action Plan, framed to implement Xi’s directive that AI be treated as an international public good, sets eight priority actions spanning data, compute, ecosystem, deployment, talent, rules, governance and ethics: high‑quality data supply, democratized/ubiquitous compute, open‑source ecosystem sharing, deep sectoral AI enablement, joint cultivation of digital‑intelligence talent, co‑development of rules and standards, collaborative security governance, and promoting AI for good. The NDRC says the plan responds to UN calls for strengthened international AI cooperation, narrowing the digital divide and using AI for sustainable development. The China Smart·Benefits the World (2026) casebook, the NDRC’s third annual edition, compiles 10 international AI cooperation projects; it reports broader country coverage, richer “AI+” application scenarios and an increased emphasis on people‑centered, benevolent AI, presenting China’s contributions to global AI development.

### 2026-07-17 12:17:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Malaysia Inflation Holds at 1.9% — The annual inflation rate in Malaysia was at 1.9% in June 2026, little changed from May's 22-month high and just below market expectations of 2.0%. Price pressures remained broad-based, with inflation steady for food & non-alcoholic beverages (at 1.4%), furnishing, household equipment & routine maintenance (at 0.4%), health (at 1.2%), and recreation (at 1.1%). Meanwhile, inflation accelerated for alcoholic beverages & tobacco (2.9% vs 2.8% in May), housing, utilities (1.4% vs 1.2%), information & communication (2.4% vs 2.1%), restaurants (2.6% vs 2.5%), and insurance & financial services (5.7% vs 4.9%). Meanwhile, transport inflation eased (2.8% vs 3.8%), while education slowed slightly (2.1% vs 2.2%). Core inflation, which excludes fuel, edged down to 1.9% from 2.0%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices were unchanged after rising 0.1% in May.

### 2026-07-17 12:16:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 2

Malaysia’s Economy Expands 5.8% in Q2 — Malaysia’s economy expanded 5.8% year-on-year in Q2 2026, accelerating from 5.4% in the previous period, according to preliminary estimates. Chief Statistician Datuk Seri Dr Mohd Uzir Mahidin said resilient domestic demand and improvements across key productive sectors continued to underpin growth, despite weakness in agriculture. Mining and quarrying rebounded strongly (10.2% vs. -2.1% in Q1), mainly driven by higher natural gas production. Manufacturing growth also accelerated (7.5% vs. 5.9%), primarily supported by increased output of electrical, electronic and optical products, as well as petroleum, chemical, rubber and plastic products. Growth remained solid but eased in services (5.4% vs. 5.6%) and construction (6.6% vs. 7.0%). Meanwhile, agriculture contracted (-3.7% vs. 2.6%), reflecting weaker output in the oil palm and fishing subsectors. In the first half of 2026, Malaysia’s economy grew 5.6%, up from 4.5% in the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-07-17 12:03:21  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Copper Falls as US-Iran Conflict Weighs — Copper futures fell below $6.2 per pound on Friday, hitting a one-week low as the escalating conflict between the US and Iran triggered a broad selloff across metals markets. The US launched multiple strikes against Iran this week, while Tehran retaliated by targeting US bases in neighboring countries, driving oil prices higher and keeping inflationary risks in focus. Investors are increasingly concerned that central banks may keep interest rates elevated for longer or even raise them further to contain inflation. Still, copper prices found some support from mounting supply concerns after a powerful storm struck top producer Chile, causing widespread power outages and significant damage. In addition, Antofagasta reported this week that first-half copper production fell 9.5% to 285,000 tonnes due to lower output at two key mines. BHP also warned that its Chilean production is expected to decline next year, while the IEA flagged tightening sulphuric acid supplies.

【出口拉動超預期  馬來西亞二季度GDP大增5.8% 通脹降溫】受服務業向好、電子產品出口強勁提振，抵消中東戰事帶來的衝擊，馬來西亞二季度經濟增速大幅超出市場預期。馬來西亞統計局週五釋出初值資料顯示，今年4至6月國內生產總值（GDP）同比增長5.8%，高於彭博調研經濟學家給出的5.2%預期中值；2026年一季度該國經濟增速為5.4%。內需旺盛疊加半導體出口景氣，對沖了中東衝突造成的擾動，市場進一步看好馬來西亞今年維持東南亞經濟高增速梯隊地位。儘管全球經濟前景不確定性上升，但半導體與人工智慧相關投資大幅擴張，持續託舉本國經濟增長。統計局表示：“2026年二季度，服務業仍是拉動經濟增長的核心動力。”礦業板塊亦實現復甦，同比增長10.2%。通脹方面，6月居民消費價格指數（CPI）回落至1.9%，而分析師此前預估該資料將維持5月2%的水平。馬來西亞依靠燃油補貼對沖國際原油高價影響，基本穩住國內消費品漲價幅度。

Rupiah Extends Gains on FDI Boost, Set for Weekly Rise — The Indonesian rupiah hovered around IDR 17,960 per U.S. dollar on Friday, extending gains for a fourth session. Sentiment was supported by foreign direct investment, which surged 27.4% yoy in Q2, pointing to resilient investor confidence. Meanwhile, the government is preparing to stabilise food prices amid El Niño-related supply risks after June headline inflation moved closer to the upper end of Bank Indonesia's target range. For the week, the currency was set for a modest 0.2% rise after prior weakness, with S&P reaffirming Indonesia's investment-grade rating with a stable outlook. However, gains were capped by higher oil prices and reports of fuel shortages in Sumatra. Traders also braced for Bank Indonesia's policy meeting next week after it delivered a cumulative 100bp rate hike in May and June. Globally, a softer U.S. dollar lent support, with the dollar index steady on Friday but on track for a weekly decline after cooler U.S. inflation tempered Federal Reserve rate hike bets.

【HKEX to accept RMB for stamp duty and trading levies】The Hong Kong SAR government gazetted the 2026 Stamp Duty (Amendment) (No. 2) Ordinance, specifying that stamp duty on RMB‑counter trades of dual‑counter securities will be calculated and paid in RMB. HKEX will permit participants to settle regulatory levies and trading‑related fees in RMB, including the SFC transaction levy, FSTB transaction levy, the investor compensation levy (currently suspended) and fees payable to the Exchange. HKEX said the measure standardizes payment currency for RMB counter trades to ease global investor use of RMB, and to help pave the way for potential inclusion of RMB counters in Stock Connect; it will continue coordinating with regulators and market participants to strengthen market infrastructure and expand the RMB product ecosystem.

### 2026-07-17 11:17:09  #宏觀 #市場

【市場監管總局：電梯交付驗證強制性國家標準公開徵求意見】從市場監管總局瞭解到，《電梯製造與安裝安全要求 第3部分：電梯交付驗證》強制性國家標準開始公開徵求意見。電梯安裝是銜接部件製造和整機投用的關鍵節點，製造單位交付前自檢是把控電梯安全的一個重要核心關口，也是築牢電梯安全防線的關鍵環節。這項國家標準，就是要統一全國電梯交付驗證專案要求，解決各地自檢標準不一、專案不全等問題。進一步強化製造單位對電梯出廠和安裝進行安全指導和監控的責任和義務。從源頭防範電梯製造和安裝的安全隱患。標準適用於新安裝的電梯，包括乘客電梯、載貨電梯、防爆電梯、消防員電梯，基本覆蓋《特種裝置目錄》內主要電梯型別，涵蓋曳引式、液壓式等不同驅動形式。明確了通用的乘客電梯和載貨電梯交付驗證的基本內容和要求。在此基礎之上，針對防爆電梯、消防員電梯等特殊梯種，單獨列出了防爆電氣部件、電氣裝置的防水保護等相應專項要求，保證了標準的適用性和專用性，滿足行業自檢工作的實際需求。

### 2026-07-17 11:14:44  #其他

【江西省鷹潭市政府原黨組成員、副市長毛建華被開除黨籍和公職】中央紀委國家監委網站7月17日通報，據江西省紀委監委訊息：經中共江西省委批准，江西省紀委監委對鷹潭市政府原黨組成員、副市長毛建華嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，毛建華身為黨員領導幹部，喪失理想信念，忘卻初心使命，對組織不忠誠不老實，對抗組織審查；無視中央八項規定精神，違規收受禮品禮金，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請和旅遊活動安排；違反組織原則，個人決定重大事項，不按規定報告個人有關事項，在組織函詢時不如實說明問題，在幹部選拔任用工作中為他人謀取利益並收受財物；廉潔底線失守，縱容默許親屬利用本人職務影響謀取私利，向從事公務的人員贈送明顯超出正常禮尚往來的禮金；違規干預和插手執法活動；家風不正，對親屬失管失教；利用職務便利，為他人在工程承攬、企業經營、崗位調整等方面謀取利益，並非法收受鉅額財物；濫用職權，造成國有資產特別重大損失。毛建華嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄、濫用職權犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經江西省紀委常委會會議研究並報中共江西省委批准，決定給予毛建華開除黨籍處分；由江西省監委給予其開除公職處分；終止其江西省第十五次黨代會代表、鷹潭市第九次黨代會代表資格；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

### 2026-07-17 11:11:03  #Trading Economics #Markets #Taiwan #Stock Market #Importance 1

TAIEX Hits 5-Week Low on Tech Selloff — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, slumped 1,900 points, or 4.3%, to 43,725 in Friday morning trade, after being almost flat in the previous session, tracking a decline on Wall Street overnight amid a sharp selloff in semiconductor stocks. The broader index hit its lowest level since June 10, mainly weighed down by electronic technology stocks, which plunged 5.0%. TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker, which accounts for more than 40% of the market's total value, slumped 3.9%, while MediaTek also plunged 6.4%. Meanwhile, Delta Electronics and Hon Hai Precision Industry slumped 5.3% and 2.3%, respectively. Rising oil prices also pressured sentiment, as concerns over inflation raised expectations of further interest rate hikes. Traders also awaited the release of a slew of data, including industrial production and retail sales for June, due next week. For the week, the index is heading for a 3.6% plunge amid higher oil prices and a global tech stock selloff.

Soybeans Retreat from 2-Month High — Soybean futures fell below $12 per bushel, easing from a two-month high reached on July 15 as improving weather prospects across the US Midwest outweighed stronger-than-expected export demand. The USDA reported net new-crop soybean sales of 1.769 million metric tons for the week ended July 9, well above market expectations, with more than 1 million tons purchased by China. The agency had also announced a series of large soybean sales to China in recent days under its daily reporting system, signaling robust demand from the world's largest importer. However, forecasts for cooler temperatures and increased rainfall across key growing areas improved crop prospects, prompting investors to lock in profits after the recent rally. Elsewhere, crude oil prices remained firm amid escalating hostilities in the Middle East and a US naval blockade in the Strait of Hormuz.

### 2026-07-17 10:45:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Japan 10-Year Yield Rises on Oil Rally — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield climbed to around 2.73% on Friday, rising for a second consecutive session as oil prices surged this week amid the escalating conflict between the US and Iran, effectively unraveling the interim peace agreement. With Japan heavily dependent on energy imports from the Middle East, the country remains especially vulnerable to supply disruptions. The benchmark yield also hovered near its highest level in 30 years amid growing concerns over Japan’s fiscal expansion plans and persistent inflationary pressures. The government recently unveiled a draft roadmap outlining more than ¥370 trillion in public and private investment through fiscal 2040 across 17 strategic sectors prioritized by the Takaichi administration. Meanwhile, a recent report showed that Japan has no immediate plans to revise the asset allocation of its state pension funds, dampening expectations for near-term support for domestic financial markets.

### 2026-07-17 10:45:32  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Corn Pulls Back from 7-Week High — Corn futures fell below $4.4 per bushel, retreating from a seven-week high reached on July 15 as investors locked in profits following a disappointing weekly US export sales and an improving weather outlook across the Midwest. The USDA reported old-crop corn export sales of 315,000 metric tons for the week ended July 9, well below market expectations of 500,000 to 1.1 million tons, while new-crop sales totaled 311,200 tons, near the lower end of forecasts of 300,000 to 1.1 million tons. Meanwhile, weather conditions became more favorable after a week of intense heat, with forecasts pointing to cooler temperatures and increased rainfall across key growing regions, boosting production prospects. Higher crude oil prices provided further support amid escalating hostilities in the Middle East and a US naval blockade in the Strait of Hormuz. However, losses were capped by expectations of tighter US supplies after the USDA lowered its 2025/26 ending stocks estimate in its July WASDE report.

【SAFE: End-March 2026 China external assets ~$12 tln; net external assets >$4 tln, second globally】At a State Council press briefing SAFE deputy director Li Bin said China has gradually developed a self-balancing external account pattern: current-account surpluses offset by capital and financial-account deficits. Funds from the current-account surplus are being deployed abroad by banks and corporates across regions, industries and financial markets. Since 2026 China’s current account has remained in surplus; in the first five months domestic entities added over $300 bln of outbound investment. At end‑March 2026 China’s external assets were about $12 tln (a record high) and net external assets exceeded $4 tln, ranking second among economies globally.

【June trade in China’s Greater Bay Area mainland nine cities tops 1.02 trillion yuan】In 1H, mainland nine cities of China’s Guangdong‑Hong Kong‑Macao Greater Bay Area recorded combined import‑export of 5.3 trillion yuan, up 20.9% YoY — 4 percentage points faster than national import‑export growth — accounting for 20.8% of China’s total and contributing 24.9% to national growth. Exports were 3.12 trillion yuan, up 11.5% YoY; imports were 2.18 trillion yuan, up 37.5% YoY. Trade hit a monthly record in January at 872.9 billion yuan (up 22% YoY); March–June each exceeded prior highs, with June trade surging 31.1% YoY to 1.02 trillion yuan. Private firms accounted for 3.57 trillion yuan in 1H trade, up 27.1% YoY, raising their share to 67.5% (up 3.2 percentage points) and contributing 83.1% of the area’s growth; foreign‑invested enterprises posted 1.56 trillion yuan, up 14.4%, representing 29.4% of the total.

Indonesia Stocks Retreat But Eye Solid Weekly Gain — Indonesia’s IDX Composite fell 26 points or 0.4% to 6,0982 in Friday morning trade, snapping a six-session gain. Sentiment turned cautious as U.S. futures tumbled after a weak Wall Street session overnight led by chip losses, while traders parsed earnings updates. Locally, concerns resurfaced over external debt, which hit USD 444 billion in May, though officials maintain it is manageable. Markets also braced for Bank Indonesia’s policy meeting next week, with focus on whether a tightening bias holds after 100bp hikes in May–June. Basic materials, healthcare, and industrials weighed, amid weakness from Hartadinata Abadi (-3.3%), Aneka Tambang (-2.3%), AKR Corp. (-1.5%), and Alamtri Minerals (-1.0%). Still, the index is tracking a second weekly gain, up about 3%, on strong Q2 FDI growth, which rose at the fastest rate since Q4 2024, driven by downstream mineral investment. Meantime, the government plans stronger steps to stabilise food prices and curb inflation amid El Niño supply risks.

【福建省委書記：晉江輝騰鞋業公司“7·9”重大火災事故，代價極為慘痛、影響極為惡劣、教訓極為深刻，要全面開展安全隱患大排查大整治行動】據《福建日報》訊息，7月16日，福建全省安全生產隱患大排查大整治工作部署推進會在福州召開。省委書記周祖翼出席並講話。周祖翼指出，晉江輝騰鞋業公司“7·9”重大火災事故，代價極為慘痛、影響極為惡劣、教訓極為深刻。周祖翼強調，要堅持問題導向，全面開展工貿及勞動密集型企業安全隱患大排查大整治行動，深查、細查、嚴查，補短板、堵漏洞、掃死角，最大限度降低事故發生機率。要深入細緻排查，全面摸清風險隱患底數。做好動員部署，緊抓排查重點，開展拉網式、地毯式全覆蓋排查，督促企業全面開展自查，實行“臺賬式管理、銷號式整改”。要堅決徹底整治，推動風險隱患動態清零。堅決抓好拆窗破網、全域“生命通道”暢通、消防設施維護提升、違章搭蓋拆除、生產作業規範等五個專項任務落實，做到問題不解決不放過，整改不徹底不放過。要嚴格監管執法，做到重拳鐵腕治違。注重改善監管執法方式，注重依法嚴肅查處，注重把握好加強安全領域監管執法與最佳化營商環境的關係，堅持“零容忍”，以“長牙齒”的監管硬措施守牢安全紅線。

EU Gas Prices Climb as Supply Risks Intensify — European natural gas prices climbed to €55.7 per MWh on Friday, the highest in nearly four months, amid increasing threats to Persian Gulf supplies as US-Iran attacks intensified. The US carried out another round of strikes on Iran on Thursday, following previous attacks that struck an unladen oil tanker bound for Iranian ports after the reimposition of a blockade. Tehran has countered with attacks targeting US military bases in neighboring countries and has reportedly called Yemen’s Houthi rebel group to shut the Red Sea export route if Washington targets Iranian power infrastructure. EU gas prices have surged over 14% this week and more than 27% so far in July, erasing an over 14% decline in Q2, as supply disruptions and stronger power generation demand constrain Europe’s ability to rebuild inventories before winter. Supply concerns were exacerbated after Qatar, the Middle East's largest LNG exporter, halted plans to boost production after one of its tankers was attacked near Hormuz.

Platinum Stays Near Multi-Month Lows — Platinum futures traded below $1,620 an ounce, pressured near their lowest levels since late November, as escalating tensions in the Middle East kept inflation concerns in focus. The US launched multiple strikes against Iran this week and reinstated a blockade on the Strait of Hormuz, while Tehran retaliated with attacks on US bases in neighboring countries. The renewed conflict sent oil prices sharply higher, reinforcing expectations that persistent energy supply disruptions could stoke inflation. Meanwhile, softer-than-expected US consumer and producer inflation data prompted markets to scale back the likelihood of a near-term Federal Reserve rate hike, supporting demand for non-yielding assets. The platinum market also draws support from a tight supply outlook as the World Platinum Investment Council continues to forecast a fourth consecutive market deficit in 2026, with demand expected to outpace supply and above-ground inventories projected to remain near historically low levels.

【尚比亞宣佈本輪霍亂疫情結束】尚比亞衛生部門16日宣佈，由於已連續4周未出現新增霍亂確診病例，該國本輪霍亂疫情結束。 贊衛生部負責技術事務的常務秘書肯尼迪·利欣皮當天在首都盧薩卡舉行的新聞釋出會上說，在這場始於2025年8月的疫情中，全國共報告1627例確診病例，其中26例死亡。利欣皮說，政府、合作伙伴、衛生工作者以及積極落實防控措施的民眾共同努力，是成功遏制此次疫情的關鍵。他呼籲民眾不要放鬆警惕，繼續採取衛生措施，防止疫情再次發生。 霍亂長期以來是尚比亞面臨的主要公共衛生挑戰之一。由於該國部分地區存在清潔飲用水供應不足和衛生設施不完善等問題，尚比亞曾多次暴發霍亂疫情。據世界衛生組織統計，2023年10月至2024年7月，尚比亞霍亂疫情造成超過700人死亡。 霍亂是由霍亂弧菌引起的急性腸道傳染病，主要經由不潔水源和食物傳染，患者常出現嘔吐、腹瀉、脫水和高燒等症狀，重症和延誤治療可致死亡。(新華社)

Australian Dollar Set for 3rd Weekly Gain — The Australian dollar fell below $0.70, but stayed near a three-week high and on track for a third consecutive weekly gain as the US dollar remained broadly weaker, while the escalating Middle East conflict weighed on risk sentiment. Softer-than-expected US consumer and producer inflation data prompted markets to scale back the likelihood of a near-term Federal Reserve rate hike, reducing support for the greenback. However, gains in the Aussie were capped by rising geopolitical risks after Brent crude surged about 17% over the past two weeks on fears that prolonged disruptions to shipping through the Strait of Hormuz could reignite global inflation and complicate the policy outlook for major central banks. In Australia, expectations for further policy tightening remained subdued, with markets pricing only a 20% chance of a rate hike in August and around 60% odds by December. Upcoming key employment and inflation data due later this month will offer further clues on the policy outlook.

China Stocks Remain Under Pressure — The Shanghai Composite fell 0.8% to a near four-month low of 3,853 on Friday, while the Shenzhen Component slid 2.1% to a more than three-month low of 14,191, as sentiment toward China's technology hardware sector turned increasingly bearish. The STAR 50 Index's fear/greed indicator, which measures selling pressure relative to buying activity, dropped to its lowest level since April 2022, as concerns over stretched valuations following a blockbuster quarter and a pullback in high-flying memory-chip shares weighed on sentiment. Meanwhile, attention turned to the World AI Conference in Shanghai, where President Xi Jinping is expected to deliver a keynote address, highlighting China's drive for technological self-sufficiency and influence in global AI standards. Technology names remained under pressure, led by Cambricon Technologies (-3%), Hygon Information Technology (-2.6%), Zhongji Innolight (-7.7%), Eoptolink Technology (-7.3%) and NAURA Technology (-2.3%).

Hong Kong Stocks Retreat Amid Global Tech Selloff — The Hang Seng Index slipped 1.0%, or 250 points, to 24,765 on Friday, tracking a broad decline across Asian markets after a selloff in US semiconductor stocks weighed on investor sentiment and reignited concerns over elevated valuations in artificial intelligence-related companies. The weakness followed overnight losses on Wall Street, where technology shares retreated amid growing skepticism over whether substantial AI-related capital spending will generate sufficient returns to justify current market valuations. Risk appetite was further dampened by higher oil prices, fueling inflation concerns and reinforcing expectations that central banks may remain cautious about easing monetary policy. Investors also stayed on the sidelines ahead of upcoming US economic data and corporate earnings reports. Notable laggards included Knowledge Atlas (-14.9%), Tencent (-1.0%), SMIC (-4.1%), Kingboard Laminates (-5.8%), and Lenovo (-2.3%).

### 2026-07-17 09:42:17  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution

【Fudan reports room‑temperature single‑electron nonvolatile memory in Science】A Fudan University team at the National Key Laboratory for Integrated Circuits and Systems and the School of Integrated Circuit and Micro‑Nano Electronics published in Science a device called QuantumFlash that uses a coplanar drain–channel–source “unified” structure to demonstrate clear single‑electron nonvolatile storage behavior at room temperature (27°C) for the first time. The team says the result overturns the conventional view that single‑electron storage is infeasible and establishes a new theoretical framework for single‑electron quantum storage that could underpin future AI‑era compute. Researchers report an end‑to‑end technology chain from materials and device innovation to high‑end chip integration and applications and plan to accelerate industrialization: they aim to form a company, engage leading AI customers, recruit strategic partners and seek social and government support to commercialize products within 1–3 years.

【我國發明的“量子快閃記憶體”技術成功構建出共面的漏極-溝道-源極“歸壹”結構，觸達理論極限】北京時間7月17日凌晨，復旦大學整合晶片與系統全國重點實驗室積體電路與微納電子創新學院周鵬-劉春森研究團隊在《科學》（Science）發表重磅成果，他們發明的“量子快閃記憶體”（Quantum Flash）技術，成功構建出共面的漏極-溝道-源極“歸壹”結構，首次在室溫（27℃）環境下清晰觀測到了單電子的非易失性儲存行為，這不僅徹底打破了“單電子儲存”無法實現的傳統認知，開創了單電子量子儲存的全新理論體系，更為AI時代算力革命奠定關鍵理論基礎。該研究將電荷儲存的資訊密度提升至理論極限，實現了“一電子一位元”，也為面向人工通用智慧（AGI）需求的高密度儲存器研發提供了新的技術基礎。目前，團隊已系統性地打通了從底層材料、器件創新到高階晶片整合與應用的全鏈條：“破曉”實現存取速度突破，“歸壹”解決密度極限，“長纓”完成了與現有CMOS矽工藝相容的原型晶片驗證。（澎湃新聞）

### 2026-07-17 09:40:09  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Gasoline Holds Near 2-Month High — Gasoline futures in the US rose above $3.30 per gallon, remaining close to their highest level since late May, driven by growing concerns over global refined fuel supplies. The collapse of the US-Iran truce and renewed tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz heightened fears of disruptions to fuel shipments from the Persian Gulf. Additionally, Ukrainian strikes on Russia's energy infrastructure continued to hamper operations at major refineries, including NORSI and Omsk. US and global refiners also reduced gasoline output in favor of higher-margin diesel and jet fuel production, while strong overseas demand pushed US fuel exports to record highs. EIA data showed gasoline inventories fell by 1.5 million barrels in the week ending July 10, leaving stockpiles about 14 million barrels below their five-year seasonal average and at their lowest level for this time of year since 2012. The US gasoline crack spread climbed to about $59 per barrel, its highest level since June 2022.

### 2026-07-17 09:39:24  #MKT News

【Survey: Japanese firms' rare-earth procurement costs up 22.3% on average; pass-through limited】Tokyo-based supply-chain risk firm Resilire’s online June survey of 400 decision-makers at manufacturers that buy rare earths and rare-earth materials found average procurement costs rose 22.3% year-on-year. About 30% of respondents reported cost increases of 20–29% (the largest share); roughly 20% reported increases of 10–19%; just over 10% reported increases of 30–39%. Most firms report limited ability to pass higher input costs to customers: among firms with rising procurement costs, 4.2% said they can almost fully pass the increase through to prices, about 10% said they cannot pass costs on at all, and more than 40% said they can absorb less than half of the added cost when combining the under-25% and 25–49% pass-through groups.

Earlier this week, RBNZ Chief Economist Paul Conway warned that renewed hostilities in the Middle East could reignite inflationary pressures, potentially prompting the central bank to tighten policy further after last week's first hike in more than three years. ### 2026-07-17 09:21:23  #MKT News #Market Themes #Chip Wars

【Tata Electronics to start wafer production at 90nm; India chip push faces headwinds】Tata Electronics plans to begin wafer production on a 90nm process at a planned large-scale fab in Dholera, Gujarat, people familiar said. 90nm is a mature node primarily used for low-end industrial and automotive chips and faces a risk of obsolescence over the next few years. The move is more conservative than Tata’s annual report for the year to March 2025, which had cited 28nm as the intended starting point for its chip-making business. The decision highlights execution and technology-capability challenges for India’s effort to build a domestic semiconductor industry from scratch.

### 2026-07-17 09:21:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 2

Dollar Set for Weekly Fall — The dollar index steadied around 100.7 on Friday but remained on track for a weekly decline, as softer-than-expected US inflation prompted traders to scale back expectations of near-term Federal Reserve rate hikes, although the escalating conflict between the US and Iran continued to fuel inflation concerns. The US launched multiple strikes against Iran this week, while Tehran retaliated by targeting US bases in neighboring countries. Economic data released earlier this week showed US consumer inflation increased less than expected in June, while producer prices unexpectedly fell. Retail sales also rose in line with forecasts, as lower gasoline prices weighed on receipts at fuel stations, while spending at motor vehicle dealers and online retailers remained strong. Meanwhile, initial jobless claims dropped to a two-month low of 208K. Markets have now largely ruled out a Fed rate hike this month, though expectations remain split over the possibility of a move in September.

### 2026-07-17 09:13:13  #市場

【五糧液普五八代領跌 白酒大單品總價連續四日重新整理近四個月新低】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，中國白酒市場主要大單品的終端零售均價7月17日整體繼續微幅回落，深陷低位連創新低。如果主要單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為9806元，較昨日小幅下跌4元，連續第六天回落，並連續四日重新整理今年3月22日以來最低。五糧液普五八代跌至776元/瓶，再創“酒價內參”自去年11月有統計以來最低。縱觀全場，核心單品的漲跌分化依然顯著，終端交投量能基本穩定，總價低位震盪下行態勢延續。今日11大白酒單品七漲四跌，贏家數量佔據明顯優勢，但輸家跌幅更具壓制力。上漲方面，國窖1573漲幅居前，上漲5元/瓶，終結此前三連跌；飛天茅臺、五糧液1618、青花汾20、洋河夢之藍M6+與習酒君品均上漲2元/瓶，飛天茅臺回補昨日全部跌幅，但仍處於近四個月次低位；五糧液1618連續兩日回升，重新站上820元關口；青花汾20自近半月低位修復，重返370元關口上方；洋河夢之藍M6+自昨日創下的近一月最低位小幅反彈，但仍處於590元關口下方；習酒君品收復昨日全部失地，重返630元關口。精品茅臺小幅上漲1元/瓶，連續兩日回升，進一步向2400元關口靠近。下跌方面，五糧液普五八代跌幅居前，下跌7元/瓶，四日累計下跌23元/瓶，價格快速下探跌至776元/瓶，再創“酒價內參”自去年11月有統計以來最低；古井貢古20與青花郎均下跌6元/瓶，其中前者再度逼近530元關口；後者再度失守690元關口。水晶劍南春小幅下跌1元/瓶，昨日反彈後弱勢回落。

### 2026-07-17 09:13:04  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Heating Oil Rises to 2-Month High — Heating oil futures in the US rose above $4.0 per gallon, hitting their highest level in two months, as escalating tensions in the Middle East deepened concerns over global supplies of refined fuels. The collapse of the US-Iran truce and renewed tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz raised fears of disruptions to fuel shipments from the Persian Gulf, while Ukrainian strikes on Russia's largest refineries continued to curb diesel exports. Seasonal refinery maintenance and years of refinery closures in the US and Europe further tightened the supply outlook. The tightening backdrop lifted US refining margins to record highs, while European diesel refining margins climbed to their highest level since at least 2011, underscoring the global shortage of refined fuels. Still, EIA data showed distillate fuel inventories rose by 4.6 million barrels in the week ending July 19, nearly offsetting the previous week's draw.

【Hyundai Motor union extends strikes to 4 hours; wage talks risk protracted standoff】The Hyundai Motor branch of the Korean Metalworkers' Union said it will extend daily strike action from 2 to 4 hours and conduct three consecutive days of 4‑hour strikes from July 20-22 after a July 16 meeting. Formal wage talks have been deadlocked for more than a week; informal working‑level contacts have not produced substantive progress. The union's principal demands are higher base pay, raising bonus payouts from 750% to 800%, reinstatement of dismissed union members and raising the retirement age; the union said it would suspend strikes if formal negotiations restart.
